- Access-blocked or fetch-failed: 5, including one government page returning HTTP 403.
- Reused evidence: 2 authority records from the supplied index; they were not counted as newly discovered Chapter 6B documents.
- Distinct organizations: CONEVAL, UNICEF, INEGI, IMCO, UNAM, Associated Press, El País, Frontiers, and the Mexican executive branch.
- Source types: official statistics/evaluations, international-organization reports, academic analysis, civil-society budget analysis, and press investigations.

Lens coverage:

- 6B.1: covered through stated welfare priorities, transfers, and poverty outcomes; direct evidence on sincerity versus political messaging remains limited.
- 6B.2: covered strongly across poverty, health, education, medicine access, and social protection.
- 6B.3: covered through children, Indigenous groups, rural populations, and women.
- 6B.4: covered through health-system design, medicine procurement, budget decisions, and administrative capacity.
- 6B.5: covered through CONEVAL monitoring, ENIGH measurement, UNICEF learning evaluation, and UNAM’s evaluation-architecture critique.
- 6B.6: covered through COVID recovery, learning loss, and medicine shortages.
- 6B.7: partially covered. Evidence raises targeting and clientelism concerns, but no decisive 2022 proof of benefits being conditioned on political loyalty was found.
- 6B.8: covered through Indigenous, child, gender, rural, and dignity-related evidence.
- 6B.9: partially covered. Evaluation institutions and Learning Camps provide positive institutional evidence, but durability and post-administration survival remain uncertain.
- 6B.10: covered with mixed evidence: lower aggregate poverty and child poverty, offset by worsening health access, persistent extreme poverty, and entrenched group disparities.

The cumulative manifest could not be written because the filesystem is read-only and the patch attempt was rejected. The cumulative manifest fallback is the supplied prior entries `AMLO22-R01`, `AMLO22-R02`, `AMLO22-R04`–`AMLO22-R08`, `AMLO22-R12`, plus new entries `WEB-6B-001` through `WEB-6B-011` above.

A further Chapter 6B-specific wave would have limited marginal value unless targeted narrowly at the residual gaps: verified 2022 evidence of welfare conditionality/clientelism, audited implementation quality for individual programs, and longitudinal evidence on whether reforms became durable institutions. Further broad searching would mostly duplicate the already well-covered mixed outcome record.

CHAPTER_CONCLUSION research-chapter-7B:
Research accounting:

- Documents discovered: approximately 40 plausible documents.
- Opened: 27 underlying pages, reports, transcripts, or PDFs.
- Newly accepted: 14 source-claim units.
- Reused evidence: 11 supplied records; not counted as newly discovered.
- Rejected: 6 items, mainly snippets, duplicated coverage, retrospective summaries, or insufficiently precise claims.
- Access-blocked: 5 pages/PDFs, including some government and legislative endpoints.
- Distinct organizations: at least 13.
- Source types: official transcripts, legislative records, constitutional/legal records, fact-checks, investigative journalism, civil-society reports, press-freedom monitoring, human-rights reports, and intergovernmental reporting.

Lens coverage:

- 7B.1: covered through fact-checks and presidential public claims; residual gap is intent behind inaccuracies.
- 7B.2: covered through repeated false/misleading claims and official doubling-down; no definitive evidence of private knowledge for every statement.
- 7B.3: covered through Casa Gris and SFP findings; no adjudicated proof of personal intervention.
- 7B.4: covered through Casa Gris, family-contract, and Pío López evidence; no finding that AMLO personally profited.
- 7B.5: covered only weakly through anti-nepotism statements and family-network reporting; direct evidence comparing appointment competence versus loyalty is a residual gap.
- 7B.6: covered through SFP and FEDE investigations, INAI-related accountability context, and documented impunity; independence and completeness remain contested.
- 7B.7: covered strongly through journalist harassment, stigmatization, military expansion, and accountability concerns.
- 7B.8: covered through the National Guard and armed-forces reforms; direct promise-by-promise documentation remains limited.
- 7B.9: covered through nepotism, family-network, and prosecutorial-accountability evidence; no comprehensive appointment/procurement dataset was available.
- 7B.10: covered strongly through the contrast between official anti-corruption/ethical rhetoric and documented misinformation, press stigmatization, impunity, and conflict-of-interest controversies.

Another broad chapter-specific wave would have limited material value now: all ten lenses have at least one defensible evidence unit, and the remaining gaps require narrowly targeted primary records—especially appointment files, procurement databases, and complete investigation dossiers—rather than more general web searching.

  item that informs several questions. Coverage is a view over these mappings, not an
  independent reason to hide mapped evidence.
- Before responding, ensure every ID in `coverage` is declared in `evidence` and
  joined through `mappings`.
- Use simple stable evidence IDs when practical. The parent normalizes harmless ID,
  mapping, and coverage inconsistencies and records warnings rather than rejecting
  otherwise useful research.
- Every retained evidence item must appear in at least one `mappings` row so chapter
  judges can see it. Every mapping and coverage `evidence_id` must exactly equal one
  declared evidence ID; never concatenate, abbreviate, or combine evidence IDs.
  Reuse evidence across every genuinely relevant lens rather than leaving it invisible.
  Complete exact lens routing before coverage bookkeeping: every selected chapter must
  receive all evidence objects that the notebook explicitly links to that chapter. It is
  acceptable to use one representative lens mapping only when the notebook records a
  chapter but no exact lens. Never replace or omit an exact researcher-recorded lens
  mapping. Do not emit only one mapping per evidence object when the notebook links it
  to several chapters or lenses.
- Use `no_evidence_found` only when the researcher explicitly records searches for
  that lens and gives a specific reason no usable evidence was found. If the notebook
  lacks an explicit disposition, use `research_blocked`; never manufacture
  `no_evidence_found` because formatting produced no mapping.
- Collect meaningful contrary evidence and explicit gaps. Do not assign scores.
- Populate `evidence_environment` from cited notebook material. Answer all twelve
  environment questions: criticism opportunity; censorship/surveillance/intimidation;
  safe reporting channels; official-statistics reliability; languages/archives searched;
  source concentration; duplicate-event risk; complaint-volume interpretation;
  relevant denominators; inherited conditions/shocks/authority; chapter-specific biases;
  and supporting E-IDs. This assessment is mandatory and may not use uncited generalities.
- Remove any researcher-written score, score range, anchor, ranking recommendation,
  or advice to a judge about scoring/null handling; record it as a normalization
  warning rather than evidence.
- Use publication_date=unknown_not_exposed_by_source only when a reliable date
  cannot be established; never use null.
- Map general repression or protest-policing evidence as context unless the claim
  explains its specific incumbent-entrenchment mechanism.
- State source-type limitations and diversity in run_profile.source_mix_note.
- Populate local_priors with one schema-valid placeholder for every selected
  methodology ID using `methodology_statuses`; the parent replaces them with its
  complete hashed provenance before validation.
- Keep mapping `relevance`, coverage `reason`, and gap prose concise. Preserve every
  required ID and relationship, but do not repeat the underlying claim in bookkeeping
  fields. Output space belongs to distinct evidence, not duplicated prose.
- The final response must be only one JSON object matching the supplied output schema.
- Echo the exact job/run/identity/period/model fields from Job input.
- Record unknown usage fields as unknown_not_exposed_by_tool; never invent usage.
- If Existing candidate is not null, this is a repair-only retry: preserve its valid
  claims and citations, but compare it against the complete inlined notebook and
  restore any defensible source-claim units it collapsed or omitted. Correct
  schema/reference defects, perform the final consistency check, and do not repeat
  research or broad file reading outside the supplied materials. A
  `recovery_evidence_catalog`, when present inside the candidate, contains cited
  evidence objects preserved from other completed formatter attempts. It is not part
  of the output schema: use it only to restore manifest-required canonical keys that
  the selected candidate omitted, assigning collision-free evidence IDs and the exact
